class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Here is a sentence from a synthetic 10-K filing: <em>&quot;Management has no plans to divest the Cascade brand.&quot;</em> Run it through a relation extractor and you will very likely get a <style data-emotion="css 1nzxbu7">.css-1nzxbu7{display:inline-block;font-family:var(--chakra-fonts-mono);-webkit-padding-start:0.2em;padding-inline-start:0.2em;-webkit-padding-end:0.2em;padding-inline-end:0.2em;border-radius:var(--chakra-radii-sm);background:var(--badge-bg);color:var(--badge-color);box-shadow:var(--badge-shadow);--badge-bg:var(--chakra-colors-gray-100);--badge-color:var(--chakra-colors-gray-800);font-size:0.84em;}.chakra-ui-dark .css-1nzxbu7:not([data-theme]),[data-theme=dark] .css-1nzxbu7:not([data-theme]),.css-1nzxbu7[data-theme=dark]{--badge-bg:rgba(226, 232, 240, 0.16);--badge-color:var(--chakra-colors-gray-200);}</style><code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">divests(Northwind, Cascade)</code> edge with a healthy confidence score, because the sentence has the surface shape of an asserted fact. It isn&#x27;t one. It&#x27;s a denial. And a knowledge graph that records it as a fact is now confidently wrong about something the source document explicitly told it was not happening.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">I&#x27;ve been running a series of experiments on entity and relation extraction for knowledge graph construction in a repo called <a href="https://github.com/johnymontana/extraction-knowledge-graph-experiments">extraction-knowledge-graph-experiments</a>. Every extractor in it is one of two shapes. <a href="https://fastino.ai/models/gliner2-5">GLiNER2.5</a> is a <strong>span model</strong>: everything it knows has to be anchored in a span of text. Claude, via the <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">claude</code> CLI, is a <strong>generative model</strong>: it answers in prose, and the work is constraining that prose back down to JSON. Both have a hole in the same place - the small semantic judgments a pipeline needs <em>between</em> its stages, which are neither spans nor essays:</p>
<style data-emotion="css 6vq53b">.css-6vq53b{padding-top:var(--chakra-space-2);padding-left:var(--chakra-space-6);margin-left:var(--chakra-space-2);margin-bottom:var(--chakra-space-4);}</style><ul class="css-6vq53b">
<style data-emotion="css bxak8j">.css-bxak8j{margin-bottom:var(--chakra-space-2);}</style><li class="css-bxak8j">Is this extracted edge actually <strong>asserted</strong>, or is it hedged, denied, or conditional?</li>
<li class="css-bxak8j">Are these two mentions the <strong>same company</strong>, or two companies with similar names?</li>
</ul>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">This post fills that hole with a third kind of model. <a href="https://typesafe.ai">TypeSafe</a> calls its flagship model <strong>Jev</strong> a <em>System One</em> model: you send state and typed questions, and you get typed answers with calibrated probabilities. No text to parse, no schema to enforce. I used it as a pipeline stage in five notebooks and measured what it bought at each one.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><style data-emotion="css pujt2y">.css-pujt2y{max-width:calc(100% - 16px);height:auto;margin-top:var(--chakra-space-6);margin-bottom:var(--chakra-space-6);-webkit-margin-start:auto;margin-inline-start:auto;-webkit-margin-end:auto;margin-inline-end:auto;display:block;padding:var(--chakra-space-2);background:var(--chakra-colors-white);border-width:1px;border-color:var(--chakra-colors-gray-200);border-radius:var(--chakra-radii-sm);box-shadow:var(--chakra-shadows-sm);-webkit-transition:all 0.2s ease-in-out;transition:all 0.2s ease-in-out;}.css-pujt2y:hover,.css-pujt2y[data-hover]{-webkit-transform:translateY(-2px);-moz-transform:translateY(-2px);-ms-transform:translateY(-2px);transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:var(--chakra-shadows-md);}</style><img src="/static/images/typesafe-jev-knowledge-graph-extraction/pipeline.svg" alt="Jev sits between the span extractor and the graph: a typed question about each candidate edge and each candidate merge, and the answers stored on the graph as data." class="css-pujt2y"/></p>
<style data-emotion="css x05lfy">.css-x05lfy{width:98%;display:-webkit-box;display:-webkit-flex;display:-ms-flexbox;display:flex;-webkit-align-items:center;-webkit-box-align:center;-ms-flex-align:center;align-items:center;position:relative;overflow:hidden;-webkit-padding-start:var(--chakra-space-3);padding-inline-start:var(--chakra-space-3);-webkit-padding-end:var(--chakra-space-4);padding-inline-end:var(--chakra-space-4);padding-top:var(--chakra-space-3);padding-bottom:var(--chakra-space-3);--alert-fg:var(--chakra-colors-blue-600);--alert-bg:var(--chakra-colors-blue-100);border-inline-start-width:4px;border-inline-start-color:var(--alert-fg);margin-top:var(--chakra-space-4);background:var(--chakra-colors-blue-50);}.chakra-ui-dark .css-x05lfy:not([data-theme]),[data-theme=dark] .css-x05lfy:not([data-theme]),.css-x05lfy[data-theme=dark]{--alert-fg:var(--chakra-colors-blue-200);--alert-bg:rgba(144, 205, 244, 0.16);}.css-x05lfy >*:first-of-type{margin-top:0;margin-left:8px;}</style><div data-status="info" role="alert" class="chakra-alert css-x05lfy">
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Everything here is real, runnable code from <a href="https://github.com/johnymontana/extraction-knowledge-graph-experiments/tree/master/notebooks">notebooks 10 through 14</a> of the experiments repo and the <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">kgx.typesafe</code> module behind them. They need a hosted API key, but every response is cached to disk, and the numbers below come from a run with no key set at all, replaying the cache.</p>
</div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>What you&#x27;ll learn:</strong> how to gate extracted edges on whether the source asserts them, why a perfect entity-resolution judge can be worth nothing, how to store judgments as graph properties so a filter becomes a <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">WHERE</code> clause, and where relation <em>selection</em> beats relation <em>generation</em>. <strong>Who this is for:</strong> developers building knowledge graph or GraphRAG pipelines who are comfortable with Python; no Cypher or TypeSafe experience needed.</p>
<style data-emotion="css qxamfn">.css-qxamfn{font-family:var(--chakra-fonts-heading);letter-spacing:var(--chakra-letterSpacings-tight);line-height:var(--chakra-lineHeights-shorter);font-size:var(--chakra-fontSizes-2xl);font-weight:var(--chakra-fontWeights-bold);margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:2em;scroll-margin-top:100px;scroll-snap-margin:100px;}.css-qxamfn[id]{pointer-events:none;}.css-qxamfn[id]:before{display:block;height:6rem;margin-top:-6rem;visibility:hidden;content:"";}.css-qxamfn[id]:hover .anchor-link{opacity:1;}</style><h2 class="chakra-heading css-qxamfn" id="the-experiments-repo-briefly"><style data-emotion="css 12m0k8p">.css-12m0k8p{pointer-events:auto;}</style><div class="css-12m0k8p">The Experiments Repo, Briefly<style data-emotion="css fyjql5">.css-fyjql5{color:var(--chakra-colors-blue-500);font-weight:var(--chakra-fontWeights-normal);outline:2px solid transparent;outline-offset:2px;opacity:0;margin-left:0.375rem;cursor:pointer;}.css-fyjql5:focus,.css-fyjql5[data-focus]{opacity:1;box-shadow:var(--chakra-shadows-outline);}</style><a href="#the-experiments-repo-briefly" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h2>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The repo is a sandbox for one question: how much of knowledge graph construction can you do with small, local, schema-driven models, and where exactly do you need something more? The workhorse is GLiNER2.5, a 194M-parameter encoder that does joint entity <em>and</em> relation extraction against an ontology you supply at runtime, on a laptop CPU. Around it sit a pipeline and synthetic corpora with hand-written gold labels, so every experiment is scored.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The corpus that matters here is <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">BUSINESS_NEWS</code>: ten documents about a handful of fictional companies, with 47 gold triples, 13 gold alias groups for entity resolution, and eleven planted <strong>modality traps</strong> - sentences like the divestiture denial above that look like facts and are not. GLiNER extracts 214 mentions and 170 candidate edges from it in about 14 seconds on a CPU.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Two findings from the nine earlier notebooks set up everything below. The repo&#x27;s hand-rolled entity resolver scores <strong>precision 1.000</strong> against the gold alias groups with recall short of it, so all the headroom is in recall. And in every comparison so far, the wins came from <em>candidate generation</em> rather than from better scoring. That one comes back three times.</p>
<h2 class="chakra-heading css-qxamfn" id="what-jev-is-in-one-request"><div class="css-12m0k8p">What Jev Is, In One Request<a href="#what-jev-is-in-one-request" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h2>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">A System One request has two parts. <strong>State</strong> is what the model looks at: a string, or a JSON object of named fields. <strong>Questions</strong> are what you want to know about it, and there are exactly three kinds:</p>
<style data-emotion="css 104nsfo">.css-104nsfo{overflow-x:scroll;width:var(--chakra-sizes-full);}</style><div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><style data-emotion="css 1yma1ta">.css-1yma1ta{text-align:left;margin-top:32px;width:var(--chakra-sizes-full);}</style><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><style data-emotion="css 19x6mbd">.css-19x6mbd{background:var(--chakra-colors-gray-50);font-weight:var(--chakra-fontWeights-semibold);padding:var(--chakra-space-2);font-size:var(--chakra-fontSizes-sm);}</style><th class="css-19x6mbd">Question</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">Asks</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">Returns</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><style data-emotion="css v9ldn8">.css-v9ldn8{padding:var(--chakra-space-2);border-top-width:1px;border-color:inherit;font-size:var(--chakra-fontSizes-sm);white-space:normal;}</style><td class="css-v9ldn8"><code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code></td><td class="css-v9ldn8">which one of these options?</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">the chosen label, a probability for every option, and a <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">confidence</code></td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8"><code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code></td><td class="css-v9ldn8">is this true?</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">a single probability of yes, between 0 and 1</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8"><code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Score</code></td><td class="css-v9ldn8">which level on this described scale?</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">an expected position, a probability per level, a <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">legend</code>, and a <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">confidence</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">That&#x27;s the whole vocabulary. Here&#x27;s a real request from notebook 10 against an acquisition press release: three questions on one state, a JSON object with named fields so a question can point at one of them by path:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-python css-0"><code class="language-python"><span class="token keyword">from</span> typesafe_sdk <span class="token keyword">import</span> Choice<span class="token punctuation">,</span> Noul<span class="token punctuation">,</span> Score

EVENT_KINDS <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;acquisition&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;One company is buying another, or part of another.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;earnings&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>    <span class="token string">&quot;Results, guidance, or a financial report.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;litigation&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>  <span class="token string">&quot;A lawsuit, investigation, or enforcement action.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;partnership&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;An alliance, joint venture, or supply agreement.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;other&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>       <span class="token string">&quot;None of the above.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
SETTLEDNESS <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;Speculative -- rumoured, or attributed to unnamed sources.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;Announced but conditional -- agreed, pending approval or closing.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;Completed -- it has happened.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">]</span>

questions <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;primary_event&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> Choice<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">&quot;What is the main corporate event `body` reports?&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        criteria<span class="token operator">=</span>EVENT_KINDS<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;names_a_price&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> Noul<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">&quot;Does `body` state a transaction price or &quot;</span>
                     <span class="token string">&quot;per-share consideration?&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;certainty&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> Score<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">&quot;How settled is the main event `body` reports?&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        criteria<span class="token operator">=</span>SETTLEDNESS<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
state <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">&quot;headline&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> doc<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token string">&quot;title&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">&quot;body&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> doc<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token string">&quot;text&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span><span class="token number">900</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
         <span class="token string">&quot;source&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> doc<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token string">&quot;source&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span>

response <span class="token operator">=</span> ts<span class="token punctuation">.</span>ask<span class="token punctuation">(</span>state<span class="token punctuation">,</span> questions<span class="token punctuation">)</span>   <span class="token comment"># ts wraps the client with a disk cache</span>
</code></pre>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The answer, in 723 tokens in and 97 out:</p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">Question</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">Answer</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">Confidence</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8"><code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">primary_event</code></td><td class="css-v9ldn8">acquisition, every other option at 0.000</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.000</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8"><code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">names_a_price</code></td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.990</td><td class="css-v9ldn8"></td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8"><code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">certainty</code></td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.000, all of the mass on &quot;announced but conditional&quot;</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.000</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">That document is too easy to show what the numbers are <em>for</em>. Ask the same three questions about document six, a wire story about an antitrust inquiry that has opened and a price war reported second-hand, and three things fall out.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>A <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code> returns a distribution, not a label.</strong> Its <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">confidence</code> summarises how <em>concentrated</em> the distribution is, and the <a href="https://docs.typesafe.ai/confidence">docs are explicit</a> that it means concentration, not correctness and not permission to act.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>A <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code> is one number.</strong> A probability of yes already carries its own confidence, and 0.5 means <em>equally likely yes or no</em>, not <em>medium</em>.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>A <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Score</code> is an expected value, and sometimes the expected value is a level nobody believes.</strong> On document six the model puts 0.70 on <em>completed</em> and 0.29 on <em>speculative</em>, almost nothing in between, and the expected position lands at 1.41 - on the one level with 0.01 of the mass. The <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">confidence</code> of 0.12 is the number that says so. An application that rounded the score and threw away the rest would record the wrong answer with no sign it had done so.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">That last point is the thesis of the whole post: <strong>read the distribution, not the label.</strong></p>
<h2 class="chakra-heading css-qxamfn" id="gating-extracted-edges-on-assertion"><div class="css-12m0k8p">Gating Extracted Edges On Assertion<a href="#gating-extracted-edges-on-assertion" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h2>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Of the 170 edges GLiNER decoded, which does the corpus actually assert? I asked two questions per edge, and they are deliberately not one question:</p>
<ul class="css-6vq53b">
<li class="css-bxak8j"><strong><code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">supported</code></strong>, a <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code>: does the document connect these two things <em>at all</em>, in any modality? This catches extraction errors.</li>
<li class="css-bxak8j"><strong><code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">status</code></strong>, a <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code> over four assertion statuses: how does the document <em>present</em> the claim? This catches the modality traps.</li>
</ul>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">One question can&#x27;t tell those two errors apart. Here&#x27;s the pair for one edge, as the notebook builds it:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-python css-0"><code class="language-python">STATUS <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;asserted&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>        <span class="token string">&quot;The text states it as a present or past fact.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;hypothetical&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>    <span class="token string">&quot;A possibility, condition or proposal, not a fact.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;negated&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>         <span class="token string">&quot;Denied, or said not to be planned or expected.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;forward_looking&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;Projected or guided to hold in the future.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
claim <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token string">&quot;&#x27;Northwind Logistics&#x27; -- acquires -&gt; &#x27;Cascade Freight Systems&#x27;&quot;</span>
meaning <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token string">&quot;Here, acquires means: the head is buying or has bought the tail.&quot;</span>

questions <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;supported&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> Noul<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string-interpolation"><span class="token string">f&quot;Does the document say anything that bears on this &quot;</span></span>
                     <span class="token string-interpolation"><span class="token string">f&quot;claim: </span><span class="token interpolation"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>claim<span class="token punctuation">}</span></span><span class="token string">? </span><span class="token interpolation"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>meaning<span class="token punctuation">}</span></span><span class="token string"> Answer yes if it discusses &quot;</span></span>
                     <span class="token string">&quot;the relationship at all, in any modality. Answer no if &quot;</span>
                     <span class="token string">&quot;it does not connect these two things.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        criteria<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">&quot;true&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>  <span class="token string">&quot;The document addresses this relationship.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
                  <span class="token string">&quot;false&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;It does not connect them, or one is absent.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;status&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> Choice<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string-interpolation"><span class="token string">f&quot;How does the document present this claim: </span><span class="token interpolation"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>claim<span class="token punctuation">}</span></span><span class="token string">? &quot;</span></span>
                     <span class="token string-interpolation"><span class="token string">f&quot;</span><span class="token interpolation"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>meaning<span class="token punctuation">}</span></span><span class="token string"> Judge only how it is presented, not &quot;</span></span>
                     <span class="token string">&quot;whether it is plausible.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        criteria<span class="token operator">=</span>STATUS<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>How the questions are batched is the interesting part.</strong> Every question in a request shares one state and is evaluated in parallel, so the unit of work is one request per <em>document</em> carrying two questions per candidate edge, and each question has to name its own edge, because question ids are never sent to the model. At 24 questions per request, the 170 edges became 340 questions in 18 requests; one question per request would have re-sent each document once per edge, about 18.8x the input tokens.</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-python css-0"><code class="language-python"><span class="token keyword">import</span> kgx<span class="token punctuation">.</span>typesafe <span class="token keyword">as</span> kts

judgments <span class="token operator">=</span> kts<span class="token punctuation">.</span>judge_edges<span class="token punctuation">(</span>ts<span class="token punctuation">,</span> graphs<span class="token punctuation">,</span> kgx<span class="token punctuation">.</span>BUSINESS_NEWS<span class="token punctuation">,</span> max_questions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">24</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
kept <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>j <span class="token keyword">for</span> j <span class="token keyword">in</span> judgments <span class="token keyword">if</span> j<span class="token punctuation">.</span>keep<span class="token punctuation">]</span>   <span class="token comment"># supported &gt;= 0.5, status asserted</span>
</code></pre>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The gate kept 114 of 170; the 56 it dropped were 31 edges the document never connected at all and 25 it connected and then hedged or denied. A few of the lowest-scoring, with GLiNER&#x27;s own confidence beside them:</p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">Edge</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">GLiNER</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">supported</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">status</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Vantage Energy Partners → competes_with → Halcyon Semiconductor</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.31</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.07</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">negated</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Torrent Microsystems → subsidiary_of → Northwind Logistics</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.70</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.12</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">negated</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Northwind Logistics → subsidiary_of → Halcyon</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.87</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.13</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">negated</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Northwind Logistics → acquires → Halcyon Semiconductor Corporation</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.82</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.19</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">negated</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Northwind Logistics Inc. → partners_with → SEC</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.90</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.28</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">negated</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">An edge can be decoded at 0.90 and still be one the document denies, because span-level confidence is about the <em>decoding</em>, not about the <em>claim</em>. One picture of all 170 edges makes the point:</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><img src="/static/images/typesafe-jev-knowledge-graph-extraction/gate-scatter.png" alt="GLiNER&#x27;s edge confidence runs the full width on both the kept and the dropped edges. The supported probability and the assertion status are what separate them." class="css-pujt2y"/></p>
<style data-emotion="css 169o8ex">.css-169o8ex{font-family:var(--chakra-fonts-heading);letter-spacing:var(--chakra-letterSpacings-tight);line-height:var(--chakra-lineHeights-short);font-size:var(--chakra-fontSizes-xl);font-weight:var(--chakra-fontWeights-bold);margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:2em;scroll-margin-top:100px;scroll-snap-margin:100px;}.css-169o8ex[id]{pointer-events:none;}.css-169o8ex[id]:before{display:block;height:6rem;margin-top:-6rem;visibility:hidden;content:"";}.css-169o8ex[id]:hover .anchor-link{opacity:1;}</style><h3 class="chakra-heading css-169o8ex" id="scoring-the-gate"><div class="css-12m0k8p">Scoring The Gate<a href="#scoring-the-gate" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h3>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The repo scores predicted triples against the 47 gold ones with an alias-tolerant matching policy, so this is comparable with every earlier notebook. One extraction pass and one inference pass, scored ungated and gated:</p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">System</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">pred / match</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">precision</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">recall</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">F1</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">GLiNER2.5, ungated</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">68 / 19</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.279</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.404</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.330</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">plus the assertion gate</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">47 / 19</td><td class="css-v9ldn8"><strong>0.404</strong></td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.404</td><td class="css-v9ldn8"><strong>0.404</strong></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>Precision went from 0.279 to 0.404 and recall did not move.</strong> Not approximately - identically. Of the 56 edges the gate removed, not one was a gold triple. A gate can only ever remove triples, so recall can&#x27;t rise; what this says is that the two questions removed only noise.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Whether that&#x27;s the right trade depends on what the graph is for, so here is the whole frontier, and it cost zero new requests: <strong>changing a threshold re-reads stored judgments; it doesn&#x27;t re-ask the model.</strong></p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><img src="/static/images/typesafe-jev-knowledge-graph-extraction/gate-frontier.png" alt="One inference pass, every gate. Sweeping the supported threshold under three status policies costs nothing, and the best F1 of 0.409 sits at a threshold of 0.6 with asserted-only status." class="css-pujt2y"/></p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The corners of that sweep matter too: <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">status</code> alone reaches F1 0.396, <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">supported</code> alone 0.396, together 0.409. For the <em>decision</em> the two signals are largely redundant; for the <em>diagnosis</em> they are not, because the 31-versus-25 split is what lets each drop be explained. That is the case for keeping both as data rather than one boolean.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">What the whole of notebook 10 cost:</p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd"></th><th class="css-19x6mbd">value</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">requests</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">159</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">questions</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">982 (6.2 per request)</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">input tokens observed</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">236,038 (about 240 per question)</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">latency per request</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">174 ms</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">GLiNER2.5 extraction of the same corpus</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">14.1 s, local, no key</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<h2 class="chakra-heading css-qxamfn" id="a-question-with-two-readings"><div class="css-12m0k8p">A Question With Two Readings<a href="#a-question-with-two-readings" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h2>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Before gating any edges, I asked about the eleven modality traps on their own, with the <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code> over four statuses and a <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code> that collapsed them to what the graph needs. My first wording of the <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code> was:</p>
<div data-status="info" role="alert" class="chakra-alert css-x05lfy">
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Can the main claim of <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">sentence</code> be recorded as a fact that currently holds?</p>
</div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">It caught <strong>8 of 11</strong> traps. The <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code> in the same request called all eleven non-asserted, and every one of the three the <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code> let through was a <strong>denial</strong>:</p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">Sentence</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">Choice</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">factual (Noul)</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Management has no plans to divest the Cascade brand.</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">negated</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.66</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">The company said it has no plans to divest...</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">negated</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.71</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Duarte has acknowledged that the chipmaker... (a hypothetical plus an explicit negation)</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">hypothetical</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.74</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Can <em>&quot;Management has no plans to divest the Cascade brand&quot;</em> be recorded as a fact that currently holds? Yes: it&#x27;s a true statement about management&#x27;s plans. The claim I wanted judged was the divestiture; the claim the model judged was the sentence, and my instruction didn&#x27;t say which to take.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Notice that the model did not come back near 0.5. The three come back at 0.66, 0.71 and 0.74, a confident answer to the reading I did not mean, and that is the primitive working as designed. <strong>A <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code> near 0.5 means equally likely yes or no. It does not mean &quot;your question was ambiguous.&quot;</strong> Ambiguity in the question has to be removed from the question; it cannot be read off the answer. The <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code> never had this problem, because naming <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">negated</code> as a distinct outcome forces the distinction the <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code> collapsed, so the fix was to make the <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code> name it too:</p>
<div data-status="info" role="alert" class="chakra-alert css-x05lfy">
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">If a knowledge graph recorded the relationship this sentence is about as a plain fact, would the graph be correct? Be careful with denials: a sentence saying something will NOT happen or is NOT planned does not make that thing a fact.</p>
</div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Same model, same sentences, same <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code> alongside: <strong>11 of 11</strong>. The three denials went from 0.66, 0.71 and 0.74 to 0.13, 0.19 and 0.15, five control sentences that <em>are</em> plain assertions stayed at 5 of 5, and the gap between the trap and control distributions opened from 0.07 to 0.63. Without the controls, a detector that answers &quot;not factual&quot; to everything would also score 11 of 11. <strong>Keep the distinction in the answer type, or state it in the question. Never fold it into a probability and hope.</strong></p>
<h2 class="chakra-heading css-qxamfn" id="a-perfect-judge-on-the-wrong-queue"><div class="css-12m0k8p">A Perfect Judge On The Wrong Queue<a href="#a-perfect-judge-on-the-wrong-queue" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h2>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The repo&#x27;s hand-rolled resolver (normalise, block, score, cluster, canonicalise) puts pairs that scored <em>below</em> the merge threshold but <em>above</em> a floor into a <strong>review band</strong>, documented in the code as the slot an LLM adjudicator fills. On this corpus the band holds 55 pairs, most of them bare prefix matches like <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Northwind</code> inside <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Northwind Logistics Inc.</code>, which the resolver scores at 0.88, just under the 0.9 threshold, because <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Northwind</code> and <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Northwind Logistics</code> should merge while <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Apple</code> and <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Apple Bank</code> should not, and the string evidence is identical. Those pairs need a decision that takes world knowledge.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">I filled the slot with TypeSafe&#x27;s own <a href="https://docs.typesafe.ai/cookbooks/entity_alignment">entity-alignment cookbook</a>, moved from beer catalogues to company mentions. The shape it recommends is not the obvious one: <strong>one <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Score</code> whose levels <em>are</em> the outcomes</strong>, instead of a probability plus two fitted thresholds. The decision rule is <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">round(score)</code>: 0 leaves the pair unlinked, 1 sends it to the curator queue, 2 merges. Three diagnostic <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Noul</code>s ride along in the same request; they don&#x27;t feed the decision, they make a disagreement with the resolver attributable rather than mysterious:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-python css-0"><code class="language-python">LINK_LEVELS <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;They refer to two different real-world entities.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>          <span class="token comment"># 0: unlinked</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;Closely related entities that may or may not be the same.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token comment"># 1: curator</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;One and the same real-world entity.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>                       <span class="token comment"># 2: merge</span>
<span class="token punctuation">]</span>

questions <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;link_state&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> Score<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">&quot;`mention_a` and `mention_b` are two mentions of a &quot;</span>
                     <span class="token string">&quot;company, each quoted with the sentence it appeared in. &quot;</span>
                     <span class="token string">&quot;How do they relate?&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        criteria<span class="token operator">=</span>LINK_LEVELS<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token comment"># diagnostics: never touch the decision, they make every verdict readable</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;same_name&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> Noul<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">&quot;Ignoring abbreviations, legal suffixes and &quot;</span>
                     <span class="token string">&quot;possessives, do the two texts name the same thing?&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;same_context&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> Noul<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">&quot;Do the two contexts describe the same entity doing &quot;</span>
                     <span class="token string">&quot;the same kind of thing?&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;abbreviation&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> Noul<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">&quot;Is one text an abbreviation, acronym, ticker symbol &quot;</span>
                     <span class="token string">&quot;or short form of the other?&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Each mention goes in with its <strong>context window</strong>, not just its surface string, because <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Apple</code> and <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Apple Bank</code> are inseparable as strings and separable in context:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-python css-0"><code class="language-python">state <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;mention_a&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
        <span class="token string">&quot;text&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;Northwind Logistics Inc.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">&quot;type&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;company&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="token string">&quot;context&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;SEATTLE -- Northwind Logistics Inc. (NASDAQ: NWL) today &quot;</span>
                   <span class="token string">&quot;announced it has entered into a definitive agreement &quot;</span>
                   <span class="token string">&quot;to acquire&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;mention_b&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
        <span class="token string">&quot;text&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;Northwind&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">&quot;type&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;company&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="token string">&quot;context&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;Priya Raman, Chief Executive Officer: Thank you. &quot;</span>
                   <span class="token string">&quot;Northwind delivered full year revenue of $2.41 billion&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">I adjudicated all 55 pairs and folded the accepted merges into the resolver&#x27;s clustering with union-find:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-python css-0"><code class="language-python">verdicts <span class="token operator">=</span> kts<span class="token punctuation">.</span>adjudicate_pairs<span class="token punctuation">(</span>ts<span class="token punctuation">,</span> band<span class="token punctuation">,</span> MENTION<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
merged <span class="token operator">=</span> kts<span class="token punctuation">.</span>apply_verdicts<span class="token punctuation">(</span>resolution<span class="token punctuation">,</span> verdicts<span class="token punctuation">)</span>   <span class="token comment"># mention_id -&gt; canon_id</span>
</code></pre>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The verdicts were flawless: twenty-eight merges on gold-labelled pairs, every one correct, and the clustering genuinely changed, from 109 clusters to 104. Then I re-scored:</p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">Resolver</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">clusters</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">precision</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">recall</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">F1</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">kgx.EntityResolver</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">109</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.0000</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.8972</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.9458</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">plus adjudicated merges</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">104</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.0000</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.8972</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.9458</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">plus merges and reviews</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">100</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.0000</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.8972</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.9458</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>Zero movement, to four decimal places.</strong> A perfect judge, and it bought nothing: every gold-labelled merge it made was <strong>already in one cluster</strong> through some third mention, and the ten merges that did change clusters are on mentions outside the gold alias groups, which the metric can&#x27;t see.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Meanwhile 41 gold-same pairs were still split, and <strong>not one of them had ever been proposed</strong> by blocking: <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">HLCN</code> against every spelling of Halcyon, and - stranger - <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Torrent Microsystems</code> against <em>itself</em>. Two identical strings in different clusters is not a string-similarity failure. It&#x27;s a <strong>type</strong> failure. The extractor types <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">HLCN</code> as <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">security</code> (a ticker symbol, which it literally is) and <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Halcyon Semiconductor</code> as <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">company</code>, and types <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Torrent Microsystems</code> both ways in different documents. Blocking is type-scoped, so none of these pairs is ever a candidate, whatever the strings say. So hand the adjudicator the eight distinct pairs blocking refused to propose.</p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">Pair</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">level</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">same_name</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">abbrev</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">outcome</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Torrent Microsystems / Torrent Microsystems</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">2.00</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.98</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.03</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">merge</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">HLCN / Halcyon Semiconductor Corporation</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.99</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.62</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.98</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">merge</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Halcyon / HLCN</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.88</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.71</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.94</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">merge</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">HLCN / Halcyon</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.49</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.68</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.83</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">review</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Eight requests. Seven merges, one review, none contradicting the gold labels, and B-cubed F1 goes from 0.9458 to <strong>1.0000 at precision 1.0000</strong>, where the repo&#x27;s best entity-resolution configurations also land, with a diagnosis attached. Read the diagnostic columns, because they <em>are</em> the diagnosis: <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Torrent Microsystems</code> against itself merges on <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">same_name</code> near 1 and <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">abbrev</code> near 0, so the type disagreement is the only reason it was ever in doubt, while <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">HLCN</code> against the full company name rides entirely on <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">abbrev</code> at 0.98, a ticker for the company, which no string metric can see. When a merge looks wrong six months from now, there is something to read.</p>
<h2 class="chakra-heading css-qxamfn" id="block-on-the-distribution-not-the-label"><div class="css-12m0k8p">Block On The Distribution, Not The Label<a href="#block-on-the-distribution-not-the-label" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h2>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Hand-feeding the pairs proves the diagnosis and leaves the pipeline as broken as it was. The cause was a type label, so the fix belongs upstream of blocking, and it&#x27;s one more <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code>. But the ontology defines <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">security</code> as <em>&quot;an issued instrument: shares, notes, bonds, or a ticker symbol,&quot;</em> so GLiNER did what it was told. The question the resolver needs answered is not <em>what kind of expression is this</em> but <em>what real-world thing does it stand for</em>, so the question asks about the <strong>referent</strong>, with the ontology&#x27;s own type descriptions as criteria:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-python css-0"><code class="language-python">Choice<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">&quot;In `document`, what kind of real-world thing does the &quot;</span>
                 <span class="token string">&quot;mention &#x27;HLCN&#x27; stand for? Judge the thing it refers to &quot;</span>
                 <span class="token string">&quot;in this document, not the form of the expression: a &quot;</span>
                 <span class="token string">&quot;ticker used to name a company stands for the company.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    criteria<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span>
        <span class="token string">&quot;company&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>  <span class="token string">&quot;A commercial issuer or private firm, &quot;</span>
                    <span class="token string">&quot;referred to by name.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="token string">&quot;security&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;An issued instrument: shares, notes, bonds, &quot;</span>
                    <span class="token string">&quot;or a ticker symbol.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="token comment"># ... the other eleven types, straight from the ontology</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Re-typing all 214 mentions took ten requests. The argmax agrees with GLiNER on <strong>192 of 214</strong>, and where it disagrees with high confidence the changes are the ones a person would make: <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Torrent Microsystems</code> and <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Vantage Energy Partners</code> from <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">security</code> to <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">company</code> at 1.00, <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">adjusted EBITDA</code> to <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">financial_metric</code>, <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">antitrust inquiry</code> to <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">litigation</code>. And <strong>the tickers don&#x27;t flip.</strong> <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">HLCN</code> stays <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">security</code> at 0.56 with <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">company</code> carrying 0.41. That is not the model failing; under an ontology that defines <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">security</code> to include ticker symbols, a ticker <em>is</em> ambiguous, and the model reports it as a distribution, which is the thing a label throws away. So use the distribution: instead of swapping in the argmax, offer each ambiguous mention to <em>every</em> type block that carries at least 25% of the probability mass, as a clone that blocking can see and that scoring must still accept:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-python css-0"><code class="language-python">retyped <span class="token operator">=</span> kts<span class="token punctuation">.</span>apply_types<span class="token punctuation">(</span>mentions<span class="token punctuation">,</span> type_verdicts<span class="token punctuation">)</span>            <span class="token comment"># argmax only</span>
res_argmax <span class="token operator">=</span> resolver<span class="token punctuation">.</span>resolve<span class="token punctuation">(</span>retyped<span class="token punctuation">)</span>

<span class="token comment"># argmax plus a clone in every type block holding &gt;= 25% of the mass</span>
soft<span class="token punctuation">,</span> clones <span class="token operator">=</span> kts<span class="token punctuation">.</span>soft_typed<span class="token punctuation">(</span>mentions<span class="token punctuation">,</span> type_verdicts<span class="token punctuation">,</span> min_prob<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">0.25</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
res_soft <span class="token operator">=</span> resolver<span class="token punctuation">.</span>resolve<span class="token punctuation">(</span>soft<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
soft_map <span class="token operator">=</span> kts<span class="token punctuation">.</span>fold_clones<span class="token punctuation">(</span>res_soft<span class="token punctuation">.</span>mention_to_canon<span class="token punctuation">,</span> clones<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">Resolution</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">clusters</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">precision</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">recall</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">F1</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">kgx.EntityResolver, GLiNER types</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">109</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.0000</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.8972</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.9458</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">re-typed, argmax</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">109</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.0000</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.9142</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.9552</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">re-typed, soft (17 clones)</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">106</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1.0000</td><td class="css-v9ldn8"><strong>1.0000</strong></td><td class="css-v9ldn8"><strong>1.0000</strong></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>The argmax recovers a little; the distribution recovers everything.</strong> Seventeen clones across 214 mentions took the <em>unchanged</em> resolver to B-cubed 1.000 at precision 1.000, with no pair hand-fed and no scoring rule touched. The clones widened candidate generation; the resolver&#x27;s own scoring still had to agree to every merge, and precision says it did. This is the <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Score</code> lesson from the first section, applied to a <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code>: a 0.56 versus 0.41 split is not a label with a wobble, it&#x27;s the model asking to be allowed both readings. Type-scoped blocking that reads only the label makes a wall of that; blocking that reads the distribution makes a candidate of it.</p>
<h3 class="chakra-heading css-169o8ex" id="refusing-to-merge"><div class="css-12m0k8p">Refusing To Merge<a href="#refusing-to-merge" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h3>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">A judge that merges everything scores perfectly on a corpus with no traps, so it needs testing on pairs that must <em>not</em> merge. The shopping corpus has twelve, <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Aurora 14</code> versus <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Aurora 14 Pro</code> and the like, and the hand-rolled resolver merges three of them. I scored them beside the corpus&#x27;s must-merge pairs, once with the pair question as written above and once with the shopping ontology&#x27;s own guideline in the instructions, which says outright that <em>&quot;&#x27;Aurora 14&#x27; and &#x27;Aurora 14 Pro&#x27; are two products, not one.&quot;</em></p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">Pair</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">gold</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">plain</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">guided</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Aurora 14 / Aurora 14 Pro</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">different</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">review (0.67)</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">reject (0.06)</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Halcyon Buds / Halcyon Buds Pro</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">different</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">reject (0.43)</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">reject (0.02)</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Aurora 14 / Aurora-14</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">same</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">merge (1.81)</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">merge (1.85)</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">Tidewater Supply / Tidewater</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">same</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">merge</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">review</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>Zero false merges on the eleven testable traps, zero false rejects on the six must-merge pairs, under both wordings.</strong> The guideline turned the adjudicator&#x27;s one hedge into a confident refusal, at a cost on the side it wasn&#x27;t written for: <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Tidewater Supply</code> versus <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Tidewater</code>, a retailer and a brand, moved from merge to review. Nothing became wrong; something became a curator&#x27;s job.</p>
<h2 class="chakra-heading css-qxamfn" id="judgments-as-graph-data"><div class="css-12m0k8p">Judgments As Graph Data<a href="#judgments-as-graph-data" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h2>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Both stages produce typed values, not prose, so they can survive into the graph as properties instead of being spent at the point of decision. Build the graph <strong>ungated</strong>, on the adjudicated clustering, and put the judgment on the edge: the gate becomes a <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">WHERE</code> clause. That&#x27;s strictly more useful, because the thing a gate throws away is also information. <em>&quot;The corpus says Northwind might acquire Cascade, pending approval&quot;</em> is a fact about the corpus, and a graph that silently dropped it can&#x27;t answer a question about it.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The repo&#x27;s Cypher writer already emits <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">confidence</code>, <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">support</code>, <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">docs</code> and <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">evidence</code> on every relationship; a dozen more lines add <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">assertion</code> and <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">supported</code> beside them. Across the 66 canonical edges: 48 asserted, 12 negated, 4 hypothetical, 2 forward-looking. Against the loaded graph, the distinction the gate drew is now a query, and so is what the gate would have thrown away:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-cypher css-0"><code class="language-cypher"><span class="token comment">// facts the corpus asserts</span>
<span class="token keyword">MATCH</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span>a<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token punctuation">[</span>r<span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token operator">-&gt;</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>b<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
<span class="token keyword">WHERE</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>assertion <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token string">&#x27;asserted&#x27;</span> <span class="token keyword">AND</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>supported <span class="token operator">&gt;=</span> <span class="token number">0.8</span>
<span class="token keyword">RETURN</span> a<span class="token punctuation">.</span>name<span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token function">type</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>r<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> b<span class="token punctuation">.</span>name<span class="token punctuation">,</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>support

<span class="token comment">// floated and never asserted - what a gate alone would have thrown away</span>
<span class="token keyword">MATCH</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span>a<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token punctuation">[</span>r<span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token operator">-&gt;</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>b<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
<span class="token keyword">WHERE</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>assertion <span class="token keyword">IN</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token string">&#x27;hypothetical&#x27;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">&#x27;forward_looking&#x27;</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
<span class="token keyword">RETURN</span> a<span class="token punctuation">.</span>name<span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token function">type</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>r<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> b<span class="token punctuation">.</span>name<span class="token punctuation">,</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>assertion<span class="token punctuation">,</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>evidence
</code></pre>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">One war story. Several sentences support one canonical edge, so their judgments have to be aggregated. My first version kept the highest-<code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">supported</code> judgment per edge and quietly lost three edges, including the headline acquisition, which is <em>hedged</em> in the press release at <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">supported</code> 0.99 and <em>asserted</em> in a later document at a lower one. Ranking on support alone picks the hedge. The aggregation now ranks on assertion first, and a three-line equivalence check (build gated, versus build ungated and filter) is the only reason this was caught instead of shipped: both paths produce the identical 46 triples.</p>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><img src="/static/images/typesafe-jev-knowledge-graph-extraction/business-news-graph.png" alt="The business-news graph with assertion-gated edges and adjudicated entities: 104 entities, 66 edges, every edge carrying its assertion status and supported probability as properties." class="css-pujt2y"/></p>
<h2 class="chakra-heading css-qxamfn" id="selection-not-generation"><div class="css-12m0k8p">Selection, Not Generation<a href="#selection-not-generation" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h2>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>Candidate generation and judgment are different jobs.</strong> Recall stayed exactly where GLiNER left it, at 0.404, because a System One model can&#x27;t <em>find</em> an edge; it has no spans and nothing to anchor. But it can <strong>select</strong>. Given two mentions the extractor already found and the short list of relations the ontology permits between their types, one <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">Choice</code> can pick the relation the document states, or <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">none</code>. So in notebook 11, code enumerates every ontology-legal, co-occurring ordered pair of GLiNER&#x27;s own mentions, and the model only chooses:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Code block" class="language-python css-0"><code class="language-python">RELATIONS <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>   <span class="token comment"># the six the ontology allows between two companies, plus none</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;subsidiary_of&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;The head is owned or controlled by the tail.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;acquires&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>      <span class="token string">&quot;The head is buying or has bought the tail.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;has_stake_in&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>  <span class="token string">&quot;The head holds an ownership interest in the tail.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;supplies&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>      <span class="token string">&quot;The head provides goods or inputs to the tail.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;partners_with&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;A stated partnership, alliance, or joint venture.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;competes_with&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;The two companies are stated rivals in a market.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string">&quot;none&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>          <span class="token string">&quot;None of these, or only in the opposite direction.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>

Choice<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    instructions<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">&quot;In `document`, which of these relationships does the text &quot;</span>
                 <span class="token string">&quot;state or discuss from &#x27;Northwind Logistics&#x27; to &#x27;Cascade &quot;</span>
                 <span class="token string">&quot;Freight Systems&#x27;, in that direction? Pick the one it &quot;</span>
                 <span class="token string">&quot;supports, or none.&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    criteria<span class="token operator">=</span>RELATIONS<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The first thing to measure is the candidate window, because it determines the whole experiment:</p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">scope</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">candidate pairs</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">requests at 24 questions</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">sentence</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">161</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">12</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">paragraph</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">295</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">16</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">document</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">1,028</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">47</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">Which scope is fair depends on how many of GLiNER&#x27;s own edges connect mentions in <em>different</em> paragraphs: <strong>130 of 170.</strong> So paragraph scope is a handicap, document scope is where the selector can see what the decoder saw, and the difference is three times the requests. At paragraph scope the selector answered <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">none</code> to 64% of the 295 candidates, which is the <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">none</code> option doing its job. Scored against gold:</p>
<div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table" class="css-104nsfo"><table class="css-1yma1ta"><thead><tr><th class="css-19x6mbd">System</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">pred / match</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">precision</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">recall</th><th class="css-19x6mbd">F1</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">GLiNER2.5 joint decoding</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">68 / 19</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.279</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.404</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.330</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">TypeSafe selection, p at least 0.5</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">89 / 19</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.213</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.404</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.279</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">union</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">123 / 25</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.203</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.532</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.294</td></tr><tr><td class="css-v9ldn8">intersection</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">27 / 8</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.296</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.170</td><td class="css-v9ldn8">0.216</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi"><strong>Same recall, half the precision, and they are not finding the same things.</strong> Both recover 19 gold triples, but only 13 are the same 13: six are found only by the selector, three <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">subject_to</code> edges to regulators among them, and six only by the decoder, every one a cross-paragraph pair the paragraph-scoped selector was never asked about. Where the two disagree on a pair both saw, the selector is often correcting the decoder: <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">partners_with(Northwind, Cascade)</code> at 0.58 becomes <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">acquires</code> at 1.00, and <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">partners_with(Vantage, Northwind)</code> at 0.97 becomes <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">supplies</code> at 1.00, both gold-correct.</p>
<h3 class="chakra-heading css-169o8ex" id="threshold-not-gate"><div class="css-12m0k8p">Threshold, Not Gate<a href="#threshold-not-gate" class="anchor-link css-fyjql5" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">#</a></div></h3>
<p class="chakra-text css-1x3gqzi">The selector&#x27;s spurious edges are not hedged claims. They&#x27;re wrong relations chosen at probability just over 0.5, a fifth of them <code class="chakra-code css-1nzxbu7" style="background-color:#f1f5f9;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">impacts</code>, the ontology&#x27;s most permissive relation.
